That is the wrong shape\n45\t/// for CLI consumers of `ready`/`list`/`search --format json`: a downstream\n46\t/// filter that deserializes into a struct with optional `assignee`/`labels`\n47\t/// fields cannot tell an omitted key from a genuinely unset value. We therefore\n48\t/// normalize the display output so `assignee` is always emitted (`null` when\n49\t/// unset) and `labels` is always an array (`[]` when empty).\n50\tfn issue_to_value(issue: &Issue) -> Value {\n51\t    let mut stripped = issue.clone();\n52\t    stripped.dependencies = vec![];\n53\t    stripped.comments = vec![];\n54\t\n55\t    let mut value = serde_json::to_value(&stripped).unwrap_or(Value::Null);\n56\t    if let Value::Object(ref mut map) = value {\n57\t        ensure_display_fields(map);\n58\t    }\n59\t    value\n60\t}\n61\t\n62\t/// Guarantee the `assignee` and `labels` keys exist on a serialized issue map.\n63\tfn ensure_display_fields(map: &mut Map<String, Value>) {\n64\t    map.entry(\"assignee\")\n65\t        .or_insert(Value::Null);\n66\t    map.entry(\"labels\")\n67\t        .or_insert_with(|| Value::Array(vec![]));\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\timpl Formatter for JsonFormatter {\n71\t    fn format_issue(&self, issue: &Issue) -> String {\n72\t        let raw = serde_json::to_string(&issue_to_value(issue)).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string());\n73\t        if Self::is_envelope_enabled() {\n74\t            Self::wrap_in_envelope(\"show\", &raw)\n75\t        } else {\n76\t            raw\n77\t        }\n78\t    }\n79\t\n80\t    fn format_issues(&self, issues: &[Issue]) -> String {\n81\t        // Emit a JSON array (not NDJSON) for consistency with the envelope contract.\n82\t        // This fixes the prior divergence where list/ready/search emitted NDJSON while\n83\t        // ready special-cased [] on empty. crate::format::{ClaimResultOutput, Formatter, StatsOutput, JsonEnvelope, ENVELOPE_VERSION};\nuse crate::model::Issue;\nuse crate::velocity::VelocityStats;\nuse serde_json::{self, Map, Value};\nuse std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};\n\n/// Global flag to enable envelope wrapping for JSON outputs.\n/// Enabled by default; can be disabled via `JsonFormatter::with_envelope_disabled()`.\nstatic ENVELOPE_ENABLED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(true);\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\npub struct JsonFormatter;\n\nimpl JsonFormatter {\n    /// Enable envelope wrapping for all JSON outputs in this process.\n    /// Once enabled, cannot be disabled (process-wide setting).\n    /// Call this early in main() before any JSON formatting occurs.\n    pub fn with_envelope_enabled() {\n        ENVELOPE_ENABLED.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n    }\n\n    /// Check if envelopes are enabled.\n    fn is_envelope_enabled() -> bool {\n        ENVELOPE_ENABLED.load(Ordering::SeqCst)\n    }\n\n    /// Wrap raw JSON string in an envelope.\n    fn wrap_in_envelope(kind: &str, raw_json: &str) -> String {\n        if let Ok(data) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(raw_json) {\n            let envelope = JsonEnvelope::new(kind, data);\n            envelope.to_json().unwrap_or_else(|_| raw_json.to_string())\n        } else {\n            // Fallback if parsing failed\n            raw_json.to_string()\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Serialize a single issue to a JSON object, stripping the bulky\n/// dependencies/comments relations for `br` compatibility, and guaranteeing\n/// that `assignee` and `labels` are always present.\n///\n/// The `Issue` struct skips `assignee` when `None` and `labels` when empty so\n/// the on-disk JSONL stays compact and `bd`-compatible. That is the wrong shape\n/// for CLI consumers of `ready`/`list`/`search --format json`: a downstream\n/// filter that deserializes into a struct with optional `assignee`/`labels`\n/// fields cannot tell an omitted key from a genuinely unset value. We therefore\n/// normalize the display output so `assignee` is always emitted (`null` when\n/// unset) and `labels` is always an array (`[]` when empty).\nfn issue_to_value(issue: &Issue) -> Value {\n    let mut stripped = issue.clone();\n    stripped.dependencies = vec![];\n    stripped.comments = vec![];\n\n    let mut value = serde_json::to_value(&stripped).unwrap_or(Value::Null);\n    if let Value::Object(ref mut map) = value {\n        ensure_display_fields(map);\n    }\n    value\n}\n\n/// Guarantee the `assignee` and `labels` keys exist on a serialized issue map.\nfn ensure_display_fields(map: &mut Map<String, Value>) {\n    map.entry(\"assignee\")\n        .or_insert(Value::Null);\n    map.entry(\"labels\")\n        .or_insert_with(|| Value::Array(vec![]));\n}\n\nimpl Formatter for JsonFormatter {\n    fn format_issue(&self, issue: &Issue) -> String {\n        let raw = serde_json::to_string(&issue_to_value(issue)).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string());\n        if Self::is_envelope_enabled() {\n            Self::wrap_in_envelope(\"show\", &raw)\n        } else {\n            raw\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn format_issues(&self, issues: &[Issue]) -> String {\n        // Emit a JSON array (not NDJSON) for consistency with the envelope contract.\n        // This fixes the prior divergence where list/ready/search emitted NDJSON while\n        // ready special-cased [] on empty. All list-like commands now emit arrays.\n        let values: Vec<Value> = issues\n            .iter()\n            .map(|issue| issue_to_value(issue))\n            .collect();\n        let raw = serde_json::to_string(&values).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"[]\".to_string());\n        if Self::is_envelope_enabled() {\n            Self::wrap_in_envelope(\"list\", &raw)\n        } else {\n            raw\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn format_error(&self, message: &str) -> String {\n        let raw = serde_json::json!({\"error\": message}).to_string();\n        if Self::is_envelope_enabled() {\n            Self::wrap_in_envelope(\"error\", &raw)\n        } else {\n            raw\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn format_claim_result(&self, result: &ClaimResultOutput) -> String {\n        let raw = raw\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn format_no_claim(&self) -> String {\n        let raw = \"{}\".to_string();\n        if Self::is_envelope_enabled() {\n            Self::wrap_in_envelope(\"claim\", &raw)\n        } else {\n            raw\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn format_stats(&self, stats: &StatsOutput) -> String {\n        let raw = serde_json::to_string(stats).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string());\n        if Self::is_envelope_enabled() {\n            Self::wrap_in_envelope(\"stats\", &raw)\n        } else {\n            raw\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn Self::wrap_in_envelope(kind, data)\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::Issue;\n    use serde_json::Value;\n\n    fn parse(line: &str) -> Value {\n Only the four count fields are always present; each\n66\t/// breakdown is `Option` and omitted (via `skip_serializing_if`) when the\n67\t/// caller did not request it (`bf stats --by-type`, …). This is the shape\n68\t/// the `Formatter` trait renders for `stats`, the same way\n69\t/// `format_claim_result` renders `ClaimResultOutput` for `claim`.\n70\t///\n71\t/// Folding the breakdowns into priority number (`\"0\"`, `\"1\"`, …) and\n78\t/// `by_assignee` uses `\"None\"` for the unassigned bucket, matching the text\n79\t/// view. `BTreeMap` gives deterministic, sorted key order.\n80\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\n81\tpub struct StatsOutput {\n82\t    pub total: usize,\n83\t    pub open: usize,\n84\t    pub in_progress: usize,\n85\t    pub closed: usize,\n86\t    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n87\t    pub by_type: Option<BTreeMap<String, i64>>,\n88\t    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n89\t    pub by_priority:
